by Artifact Studio Jewelry · SS27 Anklets

Sales image prompts

Ten prompts for each of the 40 anklets, in Etsy photo order, built from your moodboard: seamless single-colour studio, legs and feet posed like sculpture. Every anklet has its own colour world and all ten of its images stay inside it. Use the approved linen hero as the only reference image for all ten.

  • Model nano_banana_2
  • Resolution 2k
  • Aspect 1:1
  • Count 1 per prompt
  • Anklets 40
  • Prompts 400
  • Estimate about 800 credits

The ten frames

  1. 01
    Hero in the colour world

    New thumbnail on the coloured paper, charm centred with space around it. A coloured ground stands out in a search grid of white backgrounds.

  2. 02
    Legs raised, crossed

    The moodboard leg shot: legs in the air against the wall, left leg in front, cropped knee to toe so the charm stays readable.

  3. 03
    Foot on a plinth

    A bare foot rising from a plinth in the backdrop colour. The graphic, surreal frame that stops the scroll.

  4. 04
    Over a sculptural chair

    Legs over a rounded chair one shade darker than the wall, a mule hanging from the toes.

  5. 05
    Hand at the ankle, scale

    Fingertips beside the charm: the size comparison buyers need, inside the editorial look.

  6. 06
    Macro detail

    Enamel and gold rims up close on the same paper: the quality proof for the price.

  7. 07
    Postcard still life

    The family's own props (orange, sorbet, shells) on the coloured paper. Ties the piece to its story.

  8. 08
    Gift moment

    Open box in a lighter shade of the backdrop, ribbon in the accent colour.

  9. 09
    Three gold colours

    Yellow, white and rose side by side: the 27 options at a glance. Recolor visualization.

  10. 10
    In step

    Walking across the studio floor, chain swinging, charm sharp.

How each colour was chosen

  1. Gold is richest on deep jewel tones (burgundy, emerald, navy, plum, charcoal) and on warm mid-tones (apricot, blush, caramel). White, cream and yellow grounds dull it, so none are used.
  2. Enamel charms get the complementary or split-complementary colour of their main enamel: blue on apricot or coral, orange on periwinkle or aqua, green on blush, pink on mint or sage.
  3. A same-hue ground is used only with a clear value gap of 20 to 30 per cent, as in the pink moodboard set (A10).
  4. The seven solid gold charms get the deepest grounds for maximum contrast.
  5. One accent ties the frame to the charm: mules and nail polish echo an enamel colour, or stay nude when the enamel has to stand alone.
  6. All footwear is an open-back mule with no ankle strap, so nothing covers the anklet.

Check before you keep a frame

  1. The charm matches its hero: same outline, same enamel colours, gold rims, nothing added.
  2. Exactly one anklet, on the left ankle only. Reject a second chain, toe rings or a charm on the other foot.
  3. Five toes on each foot, a believable ankle bone.
  4. The charm stays small: 6 to 10 mm, never bigger than the ankle bone.
  5. No text, numbers or logos anywhere, including the gift box.
  6. The backdrop matches the swatch in every frame. A frame that drifts warmer or cooler breaks the set; regenerate it.
  7. Lay the ten side by side before uploading: if two frames share the same framing, regenerate one.

If the linen of the reference hero leaks into a frame (01, 06, 07, 09), crop the reference to the charm and a few links of chain and generate again. The new 01 replaces the linen hero as the thumbnail.

A01Wave Anklet Enamel · Luminous Blue · charm 8 mm · reference: hero A01-2
soft apricot #EDB48E Mules: cobalt blue satin · Polish: cobalt blue · Skin: medium olive Complementary: warm apricot makes the cobalt wave and the gold glow.
  1. 01

    Hero in the colour world

    Etsy thumbnail hero: this anklet with a flat wave-curl charm 8 mm, a single breaking wave whose crest curls over, filled with flat luminous cobalt blue (#1F4FD1) enamel inside a thin polished gold rim, one fine polished gold line following the curl, laid in a soft open curve directly on the seamless soft apricot paper, seen from above at a slight angle; the charm sits in sharp focus at the centre of the frame with generous empty space all around, the spring ring clasp visible at one end, one clean soft shadow; no props. No people and no hands in the frame. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: a seamless matte soft apricot paper backdrop and floor (#EDB48E); nothing but the anklet adds colour. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 400, soft directional studio key light from the upper left with one clean defined shadow, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. The anklet must be an exact copy of the anklet in the reference image: same charm outline, same colours and cell layout, same thin polished gold rims, same fine 1.0 mm cable chain, same gold jump ring; do not redesign it, do not add charms or stones; take only the anklet from the reference, never its linen background, camera angle or framing. Enamel is perfectly flat, glossy and flush inside thin polished gold rims, never domed, no gradient, no painted detail, no stones. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler. Square 1:1.
  2. 02

    Legs raised, crossed

It is exactly one anklet, worn on the left ankle of a woman with medium olive skin; the charm rests on the outer side of the ankle just below the ankle bone; the right ankle is bare; no second chain, no toe rings, no other jewelry; anatomically correct feet with five toes each. Side view, cropped from the knees to the toes so the ankle is large in frame; the charm is only 8 mm across, about as wide as a pencil is thick, and the chain is a 1 mm thread of gold, small against the ankle bone. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: a seamless matte soft apricot paper backdrop and floor (#EDB48E); the only accents are the cobalt blue satin mules and the cobalt blue nail polish; nothing else adds colour.
